Title: Brutum Fulmen: or, The Bull of Pope Pius V Concerning the
  Damnation of Q. Elizabeth (with, annexed, the Bull of Pope Paul the
  Third Against King Henry the Eighth)
Creator: Thomas Barlow (1607-1691) (prints, in English and Latin, Pius
  V's bull Regnans in Excelsis, 1570, and Paul III's bull against Henry
  VIII)
Publisher: London, 1681
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Explicit `NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT` possible-copyright-status
  confirmed directly against the Internet Archive item's own metadata,
  2026-09-25, this session.
Source: Internet Archive item brutumfulmenorbu00barluoft
  (archive.org/details/brutumfulmenorbu00barluoft), fetched directly
  (curl, raw bytes, direct datanode) from the item's own _djvu.txt
  full-text file, 2026-09-25, this session.
Provenance note: Body text unaltered including its own OCR errors
  (17th-c. long-s, and heavy garbling in the Latin marginalia in
  particular); the bull text itself (English and Latin parallel columns)
  spot-checked and legible. This is Barlow's hostile Anglican polemical
  framing embedding the bulls' actual text, not a neutral edition of the
  bulls alone - a narrow but genuine primary-source window (per this
  world's own Source Readiness Dossier, §3).
